# Skills · P · part 66

838 entries, petfish-style-rewriter to pflvg-risikoampel-und-gegenargumente. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **petfish-style-rewriter**: Use this skill when the user asks to rewrite, polish, humanize, simplify, de-AI, formalize, or express content in Petfish's writing style. It rewrites Chinese or English technical, academic, business, course, proposal, patent, and email content into a clear, structured, concise, evidence-based, engineering-oriented style. Trigger especially for phrases such as "用我的语言习惯表达", "说人话", "润色", "去AI味", "按我的风格写", or "make it sound human but still professional". ([kylecui/rswitch](https://github.com/kylecui/rswitch/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/petfish-style-rewriter) · ★1)
- **petgyebu-sprint**: petgyebu 백엔드(Spring Boot 4.0/Java 25) 스프린트 기능을 backend-engineer + qa-reviewer 2인 에이전트 팀으로 구현·검증한다. "스프린트 N 구현해줘", "F-XXXXX 구현", "이번 스프린트 진행", "다음 스프린트 시작" 요청 시 사용. 후속 작업(재실행/업데이트 필요): "스프린트 N 다시", "QA 지적 반영", "이전 스프린트 보완", "F-XXXXX만 다시 구현", "스프린트 결과 개선" 요청 시에도 반드시 이 스킬을 사용한다. ([Altair5869/petgyebu_backend](https://github.com/Altair5869/petgyebu_backend/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/petgyebu-sprint) · 2 copies)
- **pethost**: The user's own hosting for their projects (Pethost). Use whenever the user wants to deploy, host, publish, ship or put something online, such as an app, a site, a bot, an API or a database, and for everything about what already runs there, including logs, domains, environment variables and secrets, restarts, rollbacks, files and shell commands. Pethost runs Docker Compose projects on the user's rented machine. ([pethost-dev/plugin](https://github.com/pethost-dev/plugin/tree/HEAD/hermes/skills/pethost) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **pethost-sandboxes**: Linux sandboxes for AI agents (Pethost Agent Sandboxes, Firecracker microVMs) to run code and tests in isolation, give a coding agent its own computer, drive Chrome on a desktop and start ready from a template. Use whenever code should run somewhere other than the user's own computer. Not for hosting an app that stays online for people: that is the pethost skill. ([pethost-dev/plugin](https://github.com/pethost-dev/plugin/tree/HEAD/sandboxes/skills/pethost-sandboxes) · ★2)

- **pfaffian-quantum-hall-engineering**: Bottom-up engineering methodology for non-Abelian topological order using Floquet-engineered synthetic magnetic fields and Bayesian-optimized adiabatic state preparation. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/pfaffian-quantum-hall-engineering) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **pfam-based-cluster-quality-scoring**: Use when protein sequence clustering output must be quality-assessed against independent functional annotations (Pfam), e.g. to compare MMseqs2 easy-cluster runs across a sensitivity parameter sweep or to choose stable clustering settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/pfam-based-cluster-quality-scoring))
- **pfam-domain-annotation-comparison**: Use when evaluating the quality of protein sequence clusterings (e.g. from Linclust, UCLUST, CD-HIT at 90/70/50% identity) by measuring how often cluster members share identical Pfam domain annotations with their cluster representative. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/pfam-domain-annotation-comparison))
- **pfam-domain-based-feature-extraction**: Use when a genome sequence (FASTA, e.g. RefSeq bacterial genomes) needs to be converted into Pfam-domain feature vectors as the input representation for BGC detection, pfam2vec embedding, or random forest product-class classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfam-domain-based-feature-extraction))
- **pfam-domain-consensus-position-mapping**: Use when you need to project variants (e.g., ClinVar SNVs or gnomAD missense variants) from a gene/transcript like CDK13 ENST00000181839.4 onto Pfam domain consensus positions and aggregate them across homologous domain instances of a meta-domain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pfam-domain-consensus-position-mapping))
- **pfam-domain-corpus-preparation**: Use when you have unlabeled Pfam domain annotations from many genomes and need genomic-context domain embeddings (pfam2vec.csv) to feed DeepBGC or evaluate embedding biological signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfam-domain-corpus-preparation))
- **pfam-domain-domain-interaction-annotation**: Use when a transcript isoform switching event must be characterized at the interaction level — specifically when deciding which of a transcript's STRING edges are physical, domain-mediated interactions disrupted by the switch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pfam-domain-domain-interaction-annotation))
- **pfam-domain-family-homolog-grouping**: Use when Pfam-A domain annotations for Swiss-Prot human sequences must be reduced to homologous domain families (≥2 occurrences) as a precursor to meta-domain MSA construction or genetic tolerance landscape computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pfam-domain-family-homolog-grouping))
- **pfam-domain-hit-extraction**: Use when you have BGC sequences (in FASTA or GenBank format) and need to identify conserved biosynthetic domains to build a feature vector for BGC similarity clustering or to annotate gene functions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pfam-domain-hit-extraction))
- **pfam-domain-identifier-mapping**: Use when building isoform-interaction or dMDT-based networks where transcript isoform switching loses Pfam domains, and you must connect Pfam DDI pairs to gene-level PPI networks to flag disrupted interactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pfam-domain-identifier-mapping))
- **pfam-domain-inventory-tallying**: Use when building or auditing the Pfam domain inventory of a protein mapping database — i.e., when you must reproduce counts like 71,419 Pfam domains on 30,406 Swiss-Prot sequences, or populate domain-annotation tables from InterProScan output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pfam-domain-inventory-tallying))
- **pfam-domain-level-prediction-scoring**: Use when benchmarking BGC prediction methods at per-Pfam-domain resolution, e.g. comparing DeepBGC's deep-learning detector against ClusterFinder HMMs using AUC ROC on validated BGC labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfam-domain-level-prediction-scoring))
- **pfam-domain-model-curation**: Use when building or rebuilding a reduced pHMM feature library (e.g., BiG-SLiCE feature set 1) and you need to select only biosynthetically relevant Pfam families rather than scanning against all of Pfam. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfam-domain-model-curation))
- **pfam-domain-pattern-recognition**: Use when when you have a set of Biosynthetic Gene Clusters (BGCs) in GenBank format and need to identify statistically significant or topic-modeled co-occurrence patterns of Pfam domains and subPfams across multiple genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pfam-domain-pattern-recognition))
- **pfam-domain-sequence-alignment**: Use when antiSMASH GenBank outputs must be converted into ordered Pfam domain strings for BGC pairwise distance calculation, especially when complete and fragmented (or merged) BGCs make purely global or local domain alignment unreliable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfam-domain-sequence-alignment))
- **pfam-domain-sequence-encoding**: Use when training DeepBGC detection or classification models whose inputs are Pfam domain compositions (Pfam TSV from deepbgc prepare) and the sequence must be transformed into pfam2vec embeddings or a binary one-hot vector before modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfam-domain-sequence-encoding))
- **pfam-domain-tokenisation**: Use when BGCs predicted by antiSMASH must be converted into per-gene (sub)Pfam domain token lists as input for iPRESTO PRESTO-STAT/PRESTO-TOP sub-cluster mining or motif querying. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfam-domain-tokenisation))
- **pfam-domain-variant-annotation**: Use when missense/pathogenic SNVs from VCFs (gnomAD, ClinVar) must be annotated with Pfam domain assignments and consensus positions to compute domain coverage or build meta-domain tolerance profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pfam-domain-variant-annotation))
- **pfam-hmm-domain-scan**: Use when a research question asks whether a set of transcription factors (e.g. TFs occupying HOT regions) can bind single-stranded DNA directly or through their direct interaction partners, requiring domain-level annotation via PFAM HMMs plus PPI evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pfam-hmm-domain-scan))
- **pfam-protein-family-annotation**: Use when nucleotide or protein FASTA sequences of bacterial genomes need Pfam protein family profiles as features for phenotype prediction or trait association analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/pfam-protein-family-annotation))
- **pfam-scan**: Annotate the Pfam domain architecture of a protein by running hmmscan (HMMER3) against the Pfam-A profile database and parsing the --domtblout into an ordered, per-domain list (Pfam accession, name, sequence start/end, HMM coverage, E-value, bitscore, clan). Use this skill whenever the task involves Pfam domains, hmmscan/hmmsearch, HMMER domain tables, "what domains does this protein have", domain architecture, multi-domain / modular protein layout, or cross-checking a family call (e.g. a CAZyme GH/GT/PL/CE assignment) against independent HMM evidence — even if the user does not say "Pfam" or… ([Xinpeng021001/dbcan4-advanced](https://github.com/Xinpeng021001/dbcan4-advanced/tree/HEAD/skills/pfam-scan))
- **pfam2vec-embedding-vectorization**: Use when preparing Pfam-domain training or scoring inputs for the DeepBGC KerasRNN detector: rows of a Pfam TSV with in_cluster/sequence_id labels must be converted to pfam2vec.csv vectors before feeding the BiLSTM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfam2vec-embedding-vectorization))
- **pfam_enrichment_analysis**: Run PFAM/domain enrichment for maize, wheat, or rice from a gene list using species-specific EasyGS resources and streaming InterProScan preprocessing. ([lukegood/EasyGS](https://github.com/lukegood/EasyGS/tree/HEAD/easygs/skills/pfam_enrichment_analysis) · ★10)
- **pfandbriefbank-spezialdeckung**: Pfandbrief- und Spezialdeckungsfragen aus Bank-Legal-Sicht prüfen: Deckungsmasse, Treuhänder, Indeckungnahme, Immobilienfinanzierung, Register, Aufsicht und Vorstandsfreigabe. ([vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim](https://github.com/vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim/tree/HEAD/bank-rechtsabteilung/skills/pfandbriefbank-spezialdeckung))

- **pfarrbrief-katechetisch-und-lehrtreu**: Wenn es um Pfarrbrief katechetisch und lehrtreu in Römisch-katholisches Kirchenrecht CIC und Katechismus geht: erstellt den passenden Entwurf aus Sachverhalt, Norm, Beweis und Antrag; liefert einen verwertbaren Entwurf mit Anträgen, Begründung und Anlagenlogik. - **pfarrei-pfarrer-und-gremien**: Wenn es um Pfarrei, Pfarrer und Gremien in Römisch-katholisches Kirchenrecht CIC und Katechismus geht: prüft Frist, Form, Zuständigkeit, Rechtsweg und Sofortmaßnahmen; liefert eine Fristen- und Risikoampel mit Sofortschritten. - **pfarrfusion-und-vermogensubergang**: Wenn es um Pfarrfusion und Vermögensübergang in Römisch-katholisches Kirchenrecht CIC und Katechismus geht: prüft Frist, Form, Zuständigkeit, Rechtsweg und Sofortmaßnahmen; liefert eine Fristen- und Risikoampel mit Sofortschritten. ([vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim](https://github.com/vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim/tree/HEAD/roemisch-katholisches-kirchenrecht/skills/pfarrfusion-und-vermogensubergang))
- **pfas-annotation-comparison**: Use when benchmarking an automated non-targeted PFAS annotation run (e.g., FluoroMatch 2.0 output on an AFFF sample) against a previously published manual annotation, to compare classes, level-A structures, species counts, and homologous series coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-annotation-comparison))
- **pfas-annotation-confidence-scoring**: Use when you have blank-filtered LC-HRMS/MS feature lists from PFAS suspect samples (e.g. AFFF) and need to assign each feature an annotation-confidence level before reporting compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-annotation-confidence-scoring))
- **pfas-candidate-prioritization-by-scoring**: Use when you have a feature list (containing m/z, retention time, and molecular formula or neutral mass per feature) extracted from LC- or GC-HRMS data in mzML format with data-dependent acquisition, and you need to rank or flag features as likely PFAS compounds to reduce manual review burden in. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pfas-candidate-prioritization-by-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **pfas-characteristic-fragment-identification**: Use when you have centroided data-dependent acquisition (ddMS2) mzML spectra from LC- or GC-HRMS and need to prioritize putative PFAS features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pfas-characteristic-fragment-identification) · 2 copies)
- **pfas-characteristic-mass-difference-detection**: Use when you have centroided MS2 spectra (ddMS2 data in mzML format) from HRMS analysis and need to identify potential PFAS compounds among thousands of features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pfas-characteristic-mass-difference-detection) · 4 copies)
- **pfas-class-coverage-analysis**: Use when an automated PFAS annotation table (e.g. from FluoroMatch 2.0 on AFFF LC-HRMS/MS data) must be compared feature-by-feature against a published manual non-targeted annotation to quantify agreement and coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-class-coverage-analysis))
- **pfas-controle**: Adds a new PFAS business control (CTL20x) in controleMetierV2Pfas, shared contracts, PDF, UI grouping, and tests. ([MTES-MCT/verseau2](https://github.com/MTES-MCT/verseau2/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pfas-controle))
- **pfas-diagnostic-fragment-screening**: Use when ddMS2 mzML data are available and features from non-target screening must be prioritized as potential PFAS using MS2 diagnostic fragments and fragment mass differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-diagnostic-fragment-screening))
- **pfas-feature-annotation**: Use when when you have a feature list (m/z, retention time, intensity) from LC- or GC-HRMS non-target screening and need to identify PFAS-like homologous series characterized by CF₂ (14 Da) repeating units. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pfas-feature-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **pfas-feature-prioritization**: Use when you have detected features in LC- or GC-HRMS data (via pyOpenMS or custom feature tables) and need to systematically rank them for likelihood of being PFAS compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pfas-feature-prioritization) · 3 copies)
- **pfas-feature-prioritization-workflow-execution**: Use when you need to run and validate end-to-end PFΔScreen execution time on a centroided ddMS2 mzML sample (~4000 spectra) to reproduce the paper's sub-1-minute runtime claim. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-feature-prioritization-workflow-execution))
- **pfas-fragment-library-matching**: Use when LC-HRMS/MS precursor ions lack exact mass matches to catalogued PFAS but their MS/MS may contain diagnostic fluorine-containing fragments (e.g., [SF5]−, [SO3F]−), such as AFFF samples with suspected unknown PFAS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-fragment-library-matching))
- **pfas-homologous-series-detection**: Use when you have an m/z-resolved feature list from LC- or GC-HRMS analysis (either detected by pyOpenMS or provided as a custom Excel table) and need to prioritize potential PFAS compounds by identifying clusters of homologous structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pfas-homologous-series-detection) · 3 copies)
- **pfas-homologous-series-detection**: Use when you have a blank-filtered HRMS feature table (e.g., from LC-HRMS AFFF or environmental samples) and need to flag features belonging to CF2 homologous series as non-targeted evidence of PFAS identity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-homologous-series-detection))
- **pfas-homologous-series-reference-line-analysis**: Use when you have HRMS feature lists (mzML-derived or external_feature_list.xlsx) and need to visually verify separation of fluorinated features from matrix components in the MD/C vs. m/C space before applying data-reduction cutoffs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-homologous-series-reference-line-analysis))
- **pfas-mass-defect-prioritization**: Use when non-target HRMS data (mzML, ddMS2, centroided, ESI/APCI) yields thousands of features and you need to separate PFAS analytes from matrix components using mass-defect, KMD, MS2-fragment, and suspect-list criteria. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-mass-defect-prioritization))
- **pfas-mdc-mc-feature-classification**: Use when a feature list from non-target LC- or GC-HRMS data (mzML, DDA) must be reduced to plausible PFAS candidates, separating them from hydrocarbon matrix components on the MD/C vs m/C plane. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-mdc-mc-feature-classification))
- **pfas-msms-feature-annotation**: Use when processing LC-HRMS/MS acquisitions of PFAS-containing samples (e.g. AFFF, serum) where you need per-feature PFAS MS/MS evidence flags and per-round confidence-scored (score A) annotation counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pfas-msms-feature-annotation))
- **pfas-nontargeted-screening**: Use when processing LC-HRMS/MS feature tables from PFAS discovery samples (e.g., AFFF or environmental extracts) and blanks, needing to separate true PFAS features from background and annotate known, uncommon, and unknown PFAS non-targeted. Use for "run a PFLOTRAN experiment/probe/ensemble", "set up PFLOTRAN cases", "submit the PFLOTRAN array", "why did my PFLOTRAN cases fail", "score the PFLOTRAN run", or any PFLOTRAN Phase-0/Phase-5 work.
